07. REPLAY CLOCK, CANDLE VISIBILITY, CALENDARS, AND NO-LOOKAHEAD

07.1 Two explicit time concepts

Use a completed-minute replay convention, QQQ_BAR_END_REPLAY_V1.
M = the label of the latest fully revealed source candle.
D = the decision instant immediately after that minute, normally M+1 minute.
Display both when useful: "Completed candle 10:00 ET; decision time 10:01 ET".
The user's step advances decision time and consequently the visible candle.
Keep NY wall-clock semantics in input/display and use UTC instants for ordering
and ACT/365F elapsed time; never a fixed EST offset or host-local timestamp.

Canonical visibility at D:
  underlying rows: timestamp <= M, available_at <= D;
  raw sub-minute quote/trade events: source_event_at < D;
  normalized option minute <= M;
  all other inputs: known_at <= D, available by the decision event phase, and
  applicable to the requested horizon. A same-timestamp later announcement
  cannot enter an earlier decision snapshot.

Assign a source minute candle available_at=label+60 seconds unless verified source
metadata establishes an equivalent completed-minute convention. This conservative
availability adapter is explicit, not a silent shift of source timestamps. If
source stamps have incompatible semantics, require a tested adapter mapping and
a different timing profile. Never double-shift a verified end-stamped source.

This reconciles SRC-B's filter-rows-first rule with SRC-C's allowance for events
inside a fully revealed cursor minute. In a true event-time request at t, use
only events <=t and candles whose available_at<=t. Never reuse the completed-bar
special rule to reveal the rest of an unfinished minute.
The completed-interval rule applies to underlying AND option OHLCV. An option
bar's full high/low/close/volume is unavailable at its opening label; raw quote/
trade events retain their original event times. If temporal labeling cannot be
verified, return TEMPORAL_SEMANTICS_UNVERIFIED for affected capabilities.
At a session-open decision instant, no new-session candle is yet complete. Do
not reveal the 09:30 candle at 09:30; normally it first appears at 09:31. A prior
session reference remains separately stale and cannot become current spot.

07.2 Order causality at equal timestamps

An order submitted after viewing completed candle M cannot fill at an option
event earlier than D. Events exactly at D have not been revealed by the <D
snapshot and are processed on the next advance with a stable phase/sequence rule.
Use a logical event key (instant, phase, source_sequence, stable_id). Persist the
ordering policy. An opening quote already observed at D cannot be replayed as a
new fill event for an order submitted afterward. If source timing precision is
only a minute, require the next eligible update/minute and disclose uncertainty.

Example: after revealing 10:00:00-10:00:59, the user submits a spread at 10:01.
It may use a later eligible quote update, not the 10:00 high/low, a 10:00:30 bid,
or the completed candle's best price. A jump to 10:06 processes all events in
between in chronological order. A default 1-minute step is a display/control
interval, not a license to ignore intra-step events that the archive contains.

07.3 Candle construction and indicators

Filter revealed 1-minute rows first, then aggregate with buckets anchored to the
actual 09:30 session open. Use first open, max high, min low, last close, sum volume.
Mark the current higher-timeframe bucket partial until all its minutes are known.
Chart timeframe may be 1/5/15/30/60 minutes or daily; it cannot alter simulation
execution time or the underlying as-of source. Daily bars use only known RTH rows.
Indicators, warm-up data, realized returns, feature scalers, and volatility
diagnostics obey the same visibility filter. Never compute a full-day indicator
then slice it for an earlier time. Show missing slots/gaps; do not manufacture
volume or price candles to make an apparently continuous chart.

07.4 Steps and nontrading time

Default forward step: one scheduled trading minute from the selected calendar.
Count calendar slots, not observed rows; missing/excluded rows remain gaps and
invoke the gap policy, without silently lengthening elapsed time. Next observed
bar is a separate explicit command. Other controls: 5/15/30/60
trading minutes, N trading minutes, elapsed clock duration, next session open,
next eligible event, and exact date/time. Label trading-minute vs clock-duration
steps explicitly. Market closures count for interest/settlement/time decay and
model diffusion even if the UI jumps them visually.
Known weekends/holidays may be traversed according to the selected command;
missing expected sessions stop strict replay. A jump to a missing requested date
must not quietly choose a neighboring day. Opening a new inspection session at a
chosen historical point is different from advancing an existing funded account.

07.5 Product sessions and data support

Resolve dated exchange calendars, early closes, contract stop-trading times,
fixing times, exercise deadlines, and settlement business days independently.
QQQ appears in Nasdaq's group with options trading through 16:15 ET on ordinary
days. [APP-C03] The canonical source contract supplies RTH minute analysis before
16:00; do not fabricate the remaining quarter hour or substitute embedded spot.

The active default replay execution window is the intersection of verified
product eligibility and actual compatible source coverage, usually RTH only.
Record monitoring_calendar_id separately from exchange_calendar_id. P50 using
RTH-only eligible decision times must say so. A full-session theoretical reference
can be offered from a separately qualified pack, but cannot be represented as
an executable replay outcome using missing historical quotes. Expiry/fixing and
automatic exercise must not be guessed from the last stored candle.

07.6 No-lookahead extends beyond prices

Guard all of: OI publication, dividend declarations, rate observation/vintage,
contract listing, corporate actions, exchange rule effective dates, training/test
split, feature normalization, historical liquidity filters, and policy decisions.
Preloaded future observations are private to the data engine and never passed to
an inference request. A policy rollout sees only its current generated state,
not the hidden future path used by the evaluator. The actual future historical
price path is used to replay outcomes, never to compute today's P50 or choose
the action that would have won afterward.

08. DETERMINISTIC REPLAY TRANSACTION AND SYNCHRONIZATION PROTOCOL

08.1 Single writer and committed bundles

Every authoritative mutation has a monotonically increasing state_version.
ReplayController takes an immutable current state plus a validated command and
produces a deterministic next state and events. All readers consume a published
StateBundle with clock, snapshot, orders, account, positions, campaign summaries,
chart/chain projections and availability flags. A panel cannot advance on its own.

08.2 Advance algorithm

1. Validate target time, calendar support, branch, and command idempotency key.
2. Lock/serialize mutations; retain the last fully committed UI state.
3. Resolve needed data and lifecycle schedules through the target; validate
   coverage before each atomic event batch. A failure cannot half-commit a batch.
   A long advance commits the same valid chronological prefix as small steps,
   stopping at the first unresolved boundary; it does not roll back earlier
   fully committed economic events. Report the actual achieved cursor.
4. Enumerate intervening source updates, candle-availability events, pending
   order opportunities, configured exit triggers, fee/rule changes, dividends,
   exercise/assignment/expiry events, settlement, and margin-review instants.
5. Process events in stable chronological phase order. At each event, expose
   only information available then, update orders/inventory/cash, accrue/book
   costs, recompute constraints, and record its provenance.
6. Stop at a mandatory user decision or unresolved data/model/broker event. A
   large forward jump must not decide an unapproved adjustment for the user.
7. Build the final immutable as-of snapshot and authoritative account valuation.
8. Write events, cash postings, order states, and checkpoint metadata in one
   SQLite transaction, or in disclosed complete intermediate event batches for
   long advances. Roll back an incomplete batch on failure/cancellation.
9. Publish one new StateBundle on the GUI thread. Disable stale order actions.
10. Cancel/supersede old analytics; queue a risk request for the new exact state.
    Risk results may arrive later without blocking chart interaction.

08.3 Event phase order

Version the precise tie-breaking policy. Recommended phases for events sharing
an instant: scheduled contractual/rule events whose effective boundary comes
first; previously committed orders/cancellations; eligible market updates;
execution checks and fills; quantity/cash/fee postings; account valuation and
constraint checks; snapshot publication; newly submitted user commands.
Product-specific open/close/exercise boundaries override a generic phase list
only through a tested calendar/lifecycle adapter. At a close/fixing boundary,
publish the final candle/official fixing evidence before an expiry computation
that consumes it, while disallowing orders after the trading cutoff. Test these
event-specific dependencies instead of using a universal contractual-first rule.
Do not insert an order ahead
of a market event the user already observed. No inferred intra-bar price path.

08.4 Analysis version gate

Every risk request carries session_id, branch_id, state_version, snapshot_hash,
selection_version, campaign_ledger_hash, objective_hash, pack_id and rule IDs.
Publish a result only if every relevant identity still matches. Otherwise cache
it under its actual identity or discard it. A late worker response cannot replace
the analysis for a new trade or later time. Show "Calculating for <time>" while
pending; older results may remain only with their old time and a stale label.
Debounce rapid chain edits (initial target 200 ms) and allow cancellation.

08.5 Determinism across step sizes

Advancing five one-minute steps and one five-minute step must produce identical
ledger, fills, positions, cash and final valuation when the user's committed
instructions and model decision schedule are the same. UI refresh frequency
must not change economics. Any optional policy automation uses a fixed replay
redecision schedule and explicit authorization, not each paint/update event.

09. DESKTOP PANELS AND INTERACTIONS

09.1 Main window

Provide resizable/dockable panels with a persistent replay toolbar and account
summary. Restore layout independently of economic session state. Prioritize
legibility and precise labels; visual effects are not required.

Replay toolbar: QQQ, dataset/session range, completed candle, decision instant,
timezone/offset, pause/play, forward arrow, editable step value/unit, next session,
jump-to-time, branch/history controls, committed state number, and coverage status.
Show historical replay mode throughout. Never label a past simulated quote live.

Chart: OHLC candles, volume, crosshair, OHLCV/time tooltip, zoom/pan, timeframe,
current reveal boundary, optional trade-entry/exit markers and selected strikes.
Past-only autoscaling must not use future extrema. Rendering optimization may
decimate only displayed geometry; it cannot change underlying financial values.
Panning beyond the boundary shows empty future space, not hidden future candles.
Indicator overlays are optional and require causal calculation plus warm-up flags.

Options chain: expiry selector with actual calendar DTE and time to expiry,
centered strike column, calls/puts, bid/ask/mid/last, bid/ask sizes if known,
volume, oi_before OI, IV, delta/gamma/theta/vega and optional higher Greeks.
Show raw vendor versus derived model fields by source; never combine unlabeled
units. Use QAbstractTableModel/QSortFilterProxyModel with stable ContractKey rows,
batched updates and virtualized rendering. Filtering/scrolling must preserve
selected exact contracts. Sorting by P50 only applies to computed covered
candidates, never a fictitious probability for every chain row.

Ticket: each selected leg shows side, opening/closing effect, quantity, exact
contract, price assumption and quote age. Aggregate displays debit/credit,
estimated itemized costs, payoff max profit/loss, proposed BP impact, target,
deadline/cutoff, current quality, and verification status. Clicking ask adds a
buy leg; clicking bid adds a sell leg; explicit buttons support keyboard access.
Buying/selling a row only edits the draft until "Submit simulated order".
QQQ single-leg option quoting increments are $0.01 across premium levels under
the cited current exchange rule; complex-order increments need their own dated
rule. Do not impose a generic $3-premium penny-program threshold. [APP-C04]

Positions/campaigns: contracts/lots, signed quantities, average basis, marks,
gross/net realized/unrealized P&L, open orders, fees, BP/margin contribution,
original target, current conditional probabilities, and expiry/assignment flags.
Actions: analyze, select into a closing/adjustment ticket, partial close, full
close, roll, inspect cashflow ledger. A close action never edits positions directly.

Risk panel: primary hold-P50 at the requested deadline; managed net-realization
probability; original benchmarks and comparability; current Greeks/payoff risks;
joint envelope branches; representative feasible exact constructions; candidate
comparison; model domain/uncertainty; BP/loss checks; known/assumed fill basis.
Do not display independent Greek ranges as if every combination were feasible.
Optional envelope plots are presentation only; an inspectable text/table record
of each branch and its representative action is mandatory.

Account panel: economic and settled cash, NLV/mark equity, liquidation estimate,
gross realized/unrealized P&L, net P&L, costs, option/stock BP, initial/maintenance
requirements, gross collateral, working-order reserve, margin calls, and project
caps. Label estimated/unverified broker metrics with their rule profile.

History/diagnostics: orders, fills, cash postings, expiry/assignment, rule changes,
data errors, model results and exact input sources. Export a selected audit record.
Include data-status indicators next to affected controls, not only in a log file.

09.2 Draft and open-trade analysis

Maintain selection_version separately from account state_version. Editing a draft
does not reserve capital until order acceptance, does not freeze a new campaign,
and does not mutate another open trade. Hypothetical preview entry uses declared
quote-side prices; a midpoint preview is a separate theoretical view.
After fills, recompute actual M0/H0 from actual opening fills and booked basis
according to Section 11. Any pre-fill p0 is indicative and must not silently
become the post-fill benchmark if entry differs.

An existing campaign is analyzed with all historical cashflows and current exact
legs. Selecting a subset can show subposition HOLD metrics, but cannot detach
its losses from the campaign or imply the subset has a newly reset H0.
If imported history lacks original target/p0, show UNKNOWN and calculate current
conditional metrics where inputs suffice. Do not reconstruct an initial P50
from today's option chain.

09.3 Controls and accessibility

Provide keyboard shortcuts for step, pause, focus chain/ticket, undo draft leg,
and escape/back navigation. A repeated key cannot submit duplicate orders.
Use explicit text states, not color alone. Support 100%-200% scaling and scrolling
at common Windows resolutions. Disabled actions must name the blocking reason.
When paused, analysis may finish but market time/cash remain fixed. A visible
worker progress indicator must not cause automatic model building or trade action.

10. CONTRACT SELECTION AND SIMULATED ORDER EXECUTION

10.1 Supported orders and lifecycle

Initial order types: MARKET, LIMIT and PROTECTED_LIMIT, with DAY/GTC within the
session's declared coverage/horizon. The strict default is PROTECTED_LIMIT under
SIM_CONDITIONAL_RISK_CHECK_V1, explicitly displayed as a conditional simulated
limit with premium, fee, remaining-expense, capital and risk conditions. APP-C04
fully specifies these simulator-only conditions. MARKET is never silently
aliased to LIMIT: a bounded marketable order requires an explicit draft type
change. Ordinary MARKET/LIMIT needs a proved execution-exposure bound for strict
certification, or an explicitly uncertified research profile. Once its allowed
fill occurs, book it and any deficit; later adverse data or a risk failure cannot
retroactively veto it. Cancellation-before-fill is only effective at its actual
processed key, never a guarantee assumed to make a cap pass. Support cancellation
and cancel/replace as recorded events.
Optional take-profit and stop/stop-limit instructions require explicit simulation
selection and their own trigger/fill rules; they are not part of unchanged HOLD P50.
Do not implement unsupported order types as aliases of market orders.

States: DRAFT, VALIDATING, REJECTED, ACCEPTED, WORKING, PARTIALLY_FILLED,
FILLED, CANCEL_PENDING, CANCELED, EXPIRED, DATA_BLOCKED. Exact allowed transitions
and quantity conservation are unit-tested. Closing orders may never exceed the
available unreserved position quantity unless explicitly classified as reversal.
Reversal through zero splits into close and open portions for lots, fees and risk.

10.2 Default fill profile

Choose NEXT_ELIGIBLE_EVENT_NATURAL_V1:
  buy at the valid ask; sell at the valid bid;
  an order becomes eligible only after its submission event/latency boundary;
  use an actual later qualifying historical quote update;
  enforce the limit against total package debit/credit, not each leg separately;
  preserve the source timestamps and all assumptions in Fill;
  no midpoint price improvement is promised;
  no fill from option-candle high/low alone or future best price.

When only minute snapshots exist, execution has minute observation granularity.
An order may wait to the next eligible minute; no sub-minute path is invented.
Market orders remain unfilled/data-blocked if valid eligible quotes are absent.
An old carried-forward mark is not a new execution update. A buy at a zero ask,
crossed market, missing bid/ask, expired contract, or invalid tick is rejected.

For combo order signed leg changes d_i, estimated natural package cash flow is
  package_cash = -sum(d_i * m_i * p_side_i),
with ask for d_i>0 and bid for d_i<0. A credit limit L>=0 needs package_cash>=L;
a debit limit D>=0 needs -package_cash<=D. Express UI units explicitly as USD
or points per defined ratio unit; do not compare dollars with quoted points.

The single-leg quote archive cannot establish that all legs could actually fill
simultaneously as a historical complex order. The default combo simulation is
an explicit atomic-package assumption requiring coherent eligible quote inputs,
validated quantity ratios, and conservative natural-side pricing. Label these
fills SIMULATED_FROM_QUOTES, never actual historical executions. Strict observed-
liquidity mode cannot assert fills without sufficient synchronized quote/depth data.

10.3 Quantity and partial-fill assumptions

Quote sizes are optional in SRC-C. Missing depth must not mean infinite liquidity.
Use a visible execution-capability status. In the ordinary research fill profile,
full ratio-unit execution at eligible quotes is an explicitly stated size-unverified
assumption. A strict-depth profile blocks when sizes are unknown. No probability
or risk certificate may silently treat size-unverified fills as guaranteed.
If sizes are known and their semantics verified, fill integer ratio units up to
the available supported quantity and leave the remainder working. Do not reuse
the same observed size repeatedly without a replenishment rule. Real queue position
cannot be inferred from a quote snapshot or OI; no false queue-priority claims.

Apply configurable nonnegative slippage in the adverse direction only under a
named scenario; deterministic zero-added-slippage with natural-side fills is the
baseline assumption, not a guarantee of frictionless trading. Do not silently
charge a guessed spread multiplier. If stress risk assumes bounded slippage,
state and test the bound; actual market gaps need not respect it.

10.4 Rolls and adjustments

A roll contains explicit closes and opens with all prices, fees and capital effects.
Support close-then-open first: close order must finish before replacement opening
order is accepted using updated cash/risk. A failed second order leaves the
campaign flat with its realized history intact. An atomic simulated roll is a
separate declared combo policy; a non-atomic roll must check every intermediate
portfolio and temporary BP, not merely the final spread.

Partial close/reduce/add hedge/reposition modifies exact integer quantities and
updates the same campaign. Closing only a hedge may increase risk and margin;
"closing order" is not a blanket exemption from risk checks. Netting/offsets
cannot spend the same contract twice across campaigns or pending orders.

10.5 Execution and analytics separation

Selecting/marking contracts always permits analysis or a reasoned refusal.
Submitting an order requires current contract/session/data validation, permissions,
fees/BP/reservation checks and the selected campaign risk gate. A risk suggestion
is not a submitted order. Auto-exit at a configured target is optional, explicitly
recorded, and uses executable net P&L plus actual fill rules; a midpoint touch
cannot retrospectively create a closing fill.

11. LEDGER, POSITIONS, CAMPAIGN ACCOUNTING, AND P&L

11.1 Ledger as source of truth

Use immutable typed economic events plus balanced cash/inventory postings. Cached
position/account tables are projections rebuildable from the event log. Each fill,
fee, settlement, assignment, dividend, interest accrual and external cash change
has a unique idempotency key. Retry cannot post a fill or fee twice.

For an executed leg change d_i at premium p_i:
  premium_cash_i = -d_i * multiplier_i * p_i.
For any marked portfolio:
  position_value = sum(q_i * multiplier_i * mark_i) + shares * stock_mark.
At unchanged prices, cash plus signed position value is unchanged by an entry
except for actual costs/spread crossing. A short premium receipt is not instant
profit: its marked liability must be included.

Separate cash economics from cash settlement. Recommended convention:
  economic_cash = settled_cash + net_unsettled_cash_entitlements.
  equity_mark = economic_cash + signed_position_value
                + noncash_accrued_receivables - noncash_accrued_payables.
Do not add a settlement receivable twice when it is already in economic_cash.
On settlement, transfer between buckets without changing economic equity.
Post a dividend/interest accrual and its later cash payment as a transfer,
not a second profit or cost.

11.2 P&L views

gross_open_mark_pnl: remaining lots' signed mark value versus allocated entry basis.
gross_realized_pnl: realized economic results from closed lots before costs.
Exercise/assignment normally transfers option basis into the resulting stock;
it does not independently post option intrinsic cash or count a complete profit
while exposure remains. Any alternate split must prove exact cash/realized/
unrealized/equity reconciliation and specify the resulting stock basis.
net_realized_pnl: realized P&L less allocated booked commissions/fees/carry.
net_mark_pnl: gross realized+unrealized plus attributed paid/accrued nontrade
income, less expenses recognized exactly once to date. An optional trading-only
view excludes nontrade income and is explicitly named net_trading_mark_pnl.
estimated_net_liquidation_pnl: net cashflow history plus executable-side value of
remaining exposures minus estimated future close costs and pending liabilities.
account_economic_pnl: account equity minus initial equity minus net external
contributions, on a stated mark or liquidation basis.

Always label mark basis: midpoint, broker-style mark, natural liquidation estimate,
stale last-known mark, or theoretical model. Missing market quotes may permit a
separately labeled theoretical valuation within model coverage; it does not become
historical market P&L or an executable fill. If a required exposure cannot be valued,
total P&L is incomplete/null with known subtotals, not silently zero for that leg.
Tastytrade's documented midpoint/gross displays motivate a broker-comparable gross
view alongside the net economic view. [APP-C07]

11.3 Lot allocation

Default to deterministic FIFO within the exact contract/campaign, recorded in
the session. A deliberate specific-lot change is an explicit event. Store opening
and closing fees separately and allocate proportional cents with deterministic
remainder distribution. Partial-close realized+remaining unrealized totals must
reconcile to campaign/account totals. This is simulation economic accounting,
not a claim of reproducing a taxpayer's tax-lot reporting.

When net account positions offset across campaign tags, maintain both ownership
allocations and one actual net exposure. Prevent synthetic gross option inventory
from surviving when equal opposite actual contracts have closed. Campaign target
history remains separate from broker strategy-margin grouping.
Initial allocation rule: an opposite order first closes explicitly identified
owning-campaign lots; only excess quantity opens a new opposite position. Reject
ambiguous cross-campaign offsets until a full allocation/transfer protocol exists.
A transfer cannot create inventory, cash, fees, target resets or fresh risk capacity.

11.4 Campaign target and benchmark initialization

Default target definition chosen for continuity:
  M0_gross = maximum terminal gross profit from the INITIAL executed structure.
  H0 = 0.50 * M0_gross in USD, frozen once.
  HOLD event = theoretical gross campaign mark reaches this H0.
  MANAGED event = actual modeled/simulated net campaign profit is realized at or
                  above this SAME H0, with required market exposure removed and all material costs/delivery
obligations crystallized or conservatively reserved. Known future cash settlement
alone does not delay economic realization beyond its execution timestamp.

This managed target means earning half the initial gross maximum AFTER costs.
It is not half of a recomputed net maximum. Display both event descriptions.
An optional half-net-maximum objective must freeze a different target_id with a
documented initial closing-cost convention; it never silently replaces default H0.

For an atomic initial package, freeze on its completed fill. If an initial order
partially fills, the campaign is INITIALIZING until the intended ratio quantity
is completed or the user commits the filled subset/cancels the rest. Exposure,
costs and elapsed campaign time begin with the first fill. T0/T_hard were declared
before submission and never restart when initialization finishes. Do not assign
the full intended quantity's M0/H0 to a smaller filled portfolio. A non-atomic
initial legging sequence is unsupported for target initialization until its
intermediate exposures and completion policy are explicitly implemented.

Capture original fill basis, M0/H0, T0, finite T_hard, roll limits, entry snapshot,
model/rule IDs, p0_hold and p0_policy at the actual completed-initialization fill
state and its coherent as-of market snapshot, including all prior partial-fill
cashflows/costs. Preserve the submission preview separately. Never combine later
actual fill prices with an earlier submission snapshot as if known then. If an
event-time fill lacks coherent required spot/IV inputs, the benchmark is UNKNOWN;
only a separately identified earliest-valid post-fill benchmark may be offered.
Inference may finish asynchronously on frozen inputs; until then benchmark is
PENDING, not zero. If the opening state is unsupported, benchmark is UNKNOWN with
reason and no invented later reconstruction.

11.5 Current campaign value and loss capacity

Define distinct economic terms, each counted exactly once:
  C_trade = attributed premium, stock-trading and strike-delivery economic
            cashflows; excludes deposits, nontrade income and expenses.
  I_paid = attributable nontrade income already paid, such as dividends/rebates.
  I_accrued = attributable income earned but not paid.
  F_recognized = attributable expenses recognized to date, paid OR accrued,
                 with one authoritative expense-event set.
  V_mark = signed remaining mark value; V_close = executable-side close value.
  Pi_gross_mark = C_trade + I_paid + I_accrued + V_mark.
  Pi_net_liq = C_trade + I_paid + I_accrued - F_recognized
              + V_close - prospective_exit_costs.

Additional contingent obligations require a separate reserved term only if not
already recognized. Paying an accrued dividend/expense transfers buckets without
changing P&L. Paid income must not disappear when its pending receivable clears.
For exact supported same-expiry payoff G(S), the terminal economic loss bound is
  L_terminal = max(0, -inf_{S>=0}[C_trade + I_paid + I_accrued - F_recognized
                                + G(S) - F_terminal - U_unrecognized]).
Use it only when terminal fees/assumptions and deliverables are fully defined.
This expression includes realized prior losses, gains and every roll cashflow.
Do not merely take new-spread max loss or add entry premium again.
For nonterminal lifecycle/assignment risk, use Section 18's stronger certificate.

A flat campaign below H0 may reenter only within its original rules and finite
horizon. Target realization is absorbing only after an actual qualifying exit,
not a temporary theoretical touch. If obligations/stock/delivery costs remain,
the campaign is not financially complete. Economic target realization occurs
at the qualifying execution time once market exposure is removed and costs/
delivery liabilities are crystallized or conservatively reserved. Fixed known
T+1 settlement receivables/payables alone do not postpone an otherwise completed
realization beyond the target deadline. A new campaign cannot erase an earlier
session loss to replenish the account's aggregate risk budget secretly.

11.6 Account and campaign limits

Enforce account BP_CAP=5,000 across all open exposures and working-order reserves,
as well as actual supported broker availability. Do not allocate 5,000 anew to
each ticket. Campaign LOSS_CAP=1,000 is inherited. Engineering default for a
multi-campaign session: also enforce a conservative aggregate session loss cap
of 1,000 from the session's initial funded baseline, with external contributions
excluded from trading profit. This stricter aggregate scope is visible in setup.
Changing it creates a versioned objective/configuration change and branch; it
does not retroactively certify past decisions.

Use a joint account bound where validated. Otherwise define W_c for each active
campaign as a conservative lower bound on its FINAL TOTAL net campaign P&L,
including its already-realized history. Then
  W_session = completed_campaigns_realized_net_pnl + sum(active W_c)
              + lower_bound_of_unallocated_account_income_and_costs;
  L_session = max(0, -W_session).
Do not add active-campaign realized losses again. Pending orders/obligations enter
the appropriate W_c or account term exactly once. Disclose conservative separate
bounds rather than claiming unproved cross-campaign offsets.
Never average campaign P50s into account P50. Joint
success probabilities require a supported joint target and shared QQQ paths.

==============================================================================
END APPLICATION IMPLEMENTATION WORK PART 02/05
Do not report this part complete until its Section 28 milestones,
corresponding full tests and the above completion gate are evidenced.

==============================================================================
START APPLICATION IMPLEMENTATION WORK PART 03/05 — TASTYTRADE ACCOUNT RULES, QQQ LIFECYCLE, AND P50 MATHEMATICS
==============================================================================
Assigned application chapters: 12-15.
Implementation milestones: M4 and M5 (see Section 28).

Objective: Complete dated tastytrade-style commissions and buying-power profiles, account state, assignment/exercise/expiry and settlement. Implement legacy-compatible P50 mathematical definitions and as-of historical inputs/reference calculations without changing any original formula.

Required deliverables: Versioned fee/margin/risk-rule registry and test fixtures; exact supported collateral/capital effects; QQQ physical-delivery lifecycle and conservative assignment states; dated rate/dividend/IV parameter registry; pure payoff/pricing/first-hit reference implementation and reproducible comparison tests.

Completion gate: Meet Section 28.5 M4 and Section 28.6 M5 gates: AT15-AT30 and PT01-PT14; gross/net accounting reconciles; unsupported assignment and margin states abstain; no synthetic historical market values or false tastytrade equivalence; source P50 definitions and monitoring conventions remain unchanged.

BINDING RULE: This work-package checkpoint supplements the full in-file
instructions. Apply Section 01's precedence and the concrete P50-R/P50-M/APP-C
algorithms. Continue in the same codebase, preserve all passed checks, and never
expose future information to an as-of decision.

12. TASTYTRADE RULE PROFILES, FEES, AND BUYING-POWER ENGINE

12.1 Rule modes and fidelity

Implement historical_rules: apply documented rules effective at the replay time.
Implement fixed_rules_counterfactual: apply one explicitly chosen dated rule
profile to historical prices. This is useful when older broker schedules are
unavailable, but all results must say counterfactual/current-rule simulation.
Strict historical mode blocks unsupported fee/margin/lifecycle calculations rather
than quietly using a recent rate. Different modes are configurations of one app.
fixed_rules_counterfactual stores broker_rule_asof: that fixed date selects rates/
exemptions, account regime and settlement-lag rules. Actual replay dates still
determine market sessions and the business dates on which settlement falls.
historical_rules uses the applicable replay event/charge date. Save both clocks.

Each rule stores ID/version, complete numeric/formula content, effective dates,
applicable product/account/side/action, rate/min/cap, grouping/rounding/charge-date
policy, known_at if relevant, verification scope, exceptions, and missing evidence.
Distinguish verified public component rules from assumed aggregation and verified
account-level broker parity. No authenticated broker query is required by the app.

12.2 Current documented seed profile, not a historical universal default

For stock/ETF options, tastytrade publishes $1 per opening contract with a $10
opening-commission cap per leg and $0 closing commission; other fees remain.
QQQ is in this category, not the proprietary NDX/SPX index-fee category. [APP-C05]

The official fee PDF dated 2026-10-28 lists option clearing $0.10/contract, ORF
$0.02/contract, exercise/assignment $5, and stock clearing $0.0008/share. Ordinary
TAF is $0.00329/option sale contract or $0.000195/share sold, stock cap $9.79;
TAF is waived 2026-10-01 through 2026-12-31. Section 31 sales fees are $20.60 per
million from 2026-04-04. Standard debit-interest tiers span 11% to 8%; a conditional
6.75% program has separate eligibility. These fields require their dated scope;
the PDF is not a complete archive of earlier fees or account qualifications.
[APP-C05]

Exercise/request grouping is not $5 multiplied blindly by contract count. Official
help examples use $5 per leg/request, including a two-leg five-lot example totaling
$10. Validate allocation grouping for multiple nights/batches separately. [APP-C05]
No distinct forced-liquidation surcharge was established in the reviewed current
schedule. Do not repurpose the exercise/assignment fee for a market close.

12.3 Fee computation

For a verified order-leg opening cap group, cumulative opening commission is
  min(total_opening_contracts_filled * 1.00, 10.00).
Post only the increment over commission already assessed to that group. Partial
fill, cross-day, cancel/replace and separately submitted order grouping requires
broker fixtures; until then label the chosen grouping an assumption.
Closing commission is zero under this profile, not total closing cost zero.

Calculate clearing/ORF on applicable executed contract quantities. Option TAF
uses SOLD CONTRACT QUANTITY; stock TAF uses SOLD SHARE QUANTITY, with applicable
rates/caps/exemptions. Section 31 uses eligible DOLLAR SALE PROCEEDS. For ordinary
option sales, proceeds=premium*multiplier*contracts, not strike notional. Evaluate
each sale leg even when the package is net debit. Reversals split close/open
quantities. Exercise/assignment converts exposure and does not create a new
option opening commission. Preserve fee components, raw precision, posted cents,
rounding grouping, and source rule IDs in the ledger.

APP-C05 defines cumulative charge groups, partial-fill/cancel-replace behavior,
Decimal rounding and exact example totals. Use that complete profile directly.
Its explicitly designed grouping/rounding is simulation behavior; do not call it
exact account-specific broker billing. Sale-fee charge date is a rule field, not
the wall-clock time when code happens to run.

12.4 Distinct capital metrics

Compute and expose separately:
  opening premium paid/received;
  gross collateral/security requirement;
  entry BP reduction including costs;
  current initial and maintenance margin requirement;
  margin equity, maintenance excess, and SMA where applicable;
  options BP and stock BP available;
  working-order capital reserve;
  project cap remaining;
  economic max loss and risk-certified bound.

Option BP is not generally account NLV minus the sum of original maximum losses.
Initial and maintenance conventions differ; margin equity may exclude assets
that still count toward NLV. A long option's market value is not automatically
marginable stock collateral. Reg T/SMA and house rules require their own stateful
adapter. Use account-level before/after calculations, not isolated trade BPR sums.
[APP-C06]

12.5 Supported strategy component formulas

Notation: S=QQQ dollars/share, K=strike, p=option premium/mark per share,
m=verified multiplier, n=positive matched strategy units, C=total dollar entry
credit, D=total dollar debit, F=costs, w=width in dollars/share.

Long option/debit spread entry: cash consumed D+F. Do not also deduct D as locked
cash after it has already left cash; preserve the option asset and applicable
margin-equity treatment.

Eligible same-expiry credit vertical:
  gross_collateral=m*n*w;
  entry_BP_reduction=gross_collateral-C+F.
Eligible condor: w=max(put_wing_width,call_wing_width), not their sum, subject to
proper strikes, signs, matched integer ratios, same expiry and deliverable.
The wider-wing public example supports collateral; premiums remain separate.
[APP-C06]

Cash-secured put:
  gross_security=m*n*K;
  entry_BP_reduction=gross_security-C+F.
Covered call: allocate actual available delivered shares to coverage; do not
pledge the same shares twice. Stock financing/margin remains on those shares.
Unmatched ratios/legs are evaluated separately, with no invented offset.
[APP-C06]

Standard naked-option requirement component:
  OTM_call=max(K-S,0); OTM_put=max(S-K,0).
  req_call=max(m*(a*S-OTM_call+p), m*(b*S+p), floor_call).
  req_put=max(m*(a*S-OTM_put+p), m*(b*K+p), floor_put).
The documented baseline examples use a=0.20, b=0.10, floor=250 USD per standard
contract; effective account/underlying parameters and put cash-security floor
conventions control actual use. Opening uses the relevant entry premium;
maintenance uses the applicable current option value. These naked-position
components are inspection-only under the default account. APP-C06 supplies the
complete account available-BP algorithm for its supported structures. [APP-C06; APP-C08]

Never mix a premium-inclusive requirement with an independent identical option
liability deduction. Verify the basis with account-level before/after examples.
Naked calls and other unbounded/uncertifiable structures fail the default strict
P50 loss gate even if a broker margin formula can be computed for inspection.

Long stock baseline initial/maintenance percentages and short-stock floors are
dated profile parameters. Standard examples are 50%/25% long and 50%/30% short,
subject to house increases; current short-stock initial equity may have a $10/share
floor. [APP-C06; APP-C08] Do not assume these are an immutable QQQ requirement.
Portfolio margin, arbitrary strangle offsets, mixed expiry pairing and unusual
deliverables return UNSUPPORTED_MARGIN_STRUCTURE until separately validated.

12.6 Account grouping and reservations

Net exact positions, then allocate compatible collateral/hedges deterministically.
Keep a reproducible grouping explanation. Never spend one long option in two
spreads. Recompute when any leg closes, is assigned, expires, or a rule changes.
Preserve cross-campaign accounting allocation separately from actual margin pairing.
Compare account_before and account_after for every order/fill and temporary roll
state. Pending orders reserve plausible adverse-case incremental capital under
the declared fill bounds, released on cancel/expiry or converted on fill.
Two simultaneously accepted orders cannot both spend the same free BP.
Use the fully defined bounded component-allocation and cash-collateral algorithm
in APP-C06. It reports its own profile identity and every allocated hedge, without
claiming to reconstruct an undisclosed broker grouping algorithm.

12.7 Margin calls, day trading, cash restrictions and rule changes

Tastytrade reports adoption of the replacement intraday-margin framework on
2026-06-04, removing its old PDT count/$25,000 minimum while retaining BP and
margin controls. Standard margin privileges still require $2,000 marginable
equity. [APP-C08] Earlier replay dates need the historic PDT profile; do not count
every multi-leg click as a day trade without a validated counting policy.
[APP-C08]

Represent margin deficits, deadlines, account restrictions, and house increases
as dated state transitions. APP-C08 defines the complete supported conservative deficit state machine,
including immediate new-risk blocking, five/fifteen-business-day audit states
and an explicitly optional restriction scenario. Do not substitute an incomplete
reconstruction of discretionary broker enforcement.
[APP-C08] A deterministic forced-liquidation rule is a simulator scenario, not
proof of what a risk desk would have done.

Cash accounts use the fully defined SETTLED_CASH_ONLY_V1 funding-lot algorithm
in APP-C07. Its stricter settled-funds requirement is visible and never described
as every transaction permitted by a broker cash account.
Settlement calendars include banking differences and historical stock-lag changes.
[APP-C03; APP-C07]

13. QQQ EXERCISE, ASSIGNMENT, EXPIRY, DIVIDENDS, AND SETTLEMENT

13.1 Lifecycle is mandatory, not just an expiry payoff chart

QQQ ETF options require American exercise/physical-delivery semantics with exact
metadata. An exercise-aware theoretical pricer does not simulate allocation of
actual short assignments. Historical quotes/OI cannot reveal the user's assignment
outcome. Use a documented simulation scenario and independent seed where random
allocation is modeled; strict risk certification includes adverse feasible cases.
[APP-C08]

For each exercised/assigned quantity n and multiplier m:
  long call exercise: add n*m shares, subtract n*m*K cash;
  long put exercise: subtract n*m shares, add n*m*K cash;
  short call assignment: subtract n*m shares, add n*m*K cash;
  short put assignment: add n*m shares, subtract n*m*K cash.
Remove only the affected option quantity. Preserve other hedge legs. Post fees,
funding/settlement obligations, borrow exposure and revised margin. Do not also
cash-settle intrinsic value, which would double count the option's economics.
Exercise transfers basis according to the documented lot convention, not a
fictional profitable option close followed by a free stock position.

13.2 Expiry and official close

The $0.01 in-the-money exercise-by-exception convention uses an applicable official
underlying close and allows contrary instructions. It is a moneyness threshold,
not the $0.01 total-position POP threshold. [APP-C08] A 15:59 candle close may be
a proxy but is not automatically a verified official closing auction price.
If official close/after-hours/instructions are unavailable, record explicit
assumptions or stop strict lifecycle replay; no fabricated observed outcome.

Model expiry trading cutoff, fixing reference, DNE/exercise instruction cutoff,
assignment allocation/notice, stock exposure effective time, and settlement date
separately. Economic risk can exist before an assignment notice becomes visible.
Long-option DNE eligibility is distinct from exchange trading cutoff. APP-C08
defines a complete conservative simulated cutoff and a typed exact-cutoff input;
late requests are uncertain rather than automatically effective. [APP-C08]
Short positions cannot issue DNE to prevent another holder's exercise.

13.3 Early assignment, dividends and residual risk

Support deterministic exercise instructions for long options and explicit early-
assignment scenarios for shorts. An economic exercise boundary may inform a
scenario but is not an observed assignment probability. Deep ITM/low extrinsic/
dividend conditions matter; never assume early assignment cannot occur because
the position was entered as a spread. [APP-C08]

If a short is assigned, the long hedge remains unless separately exercised/sold.
Preserve that hedge, stock, premium basis and obligations in the same campaign.
Stock funding may exceed the apparent option-spread BP. Invalid/unsupported
exposure must be represented and managed, not dropped from the account.
If stock exposure is outside the risk pack, return UNSUPPORTED_EXPOSURE while
continuing accurate bookkeeping and supported close/remediation previews.

Require dated declaration/ex/record/pay dates and amounts for dividends when
needed. Only announced/known information enters an as-of model. Entitlement,
accrual and cash posting are separate; short stock can owe a payment. [APP-C08]
The inherited continuous-q HOLD model is explicitly an approximation; do not
subtract a discrete dividend again in the same model without a new version.
Actual historical underlying bars already reflect the historical price path;
do not manually subtract the dividend from replay prices a second time.

13.4 Broker intervention

Tastytrade can intervene based on risk and circumstances and does not promise one
universal exact close time. It may reduce/close or hedge; it need not save a
position from exercise risk. [APP-C08] Provide a named deterministic simulated
intervention policy and adverse scenarios, never call it a reconstructed fact.
A forced-close instruction can fail for missing liquidity; a limit cap is not
an assured stop. Account for residual partial fills and later assignment.

13.5 Settlement and financing

Option premium settlement and underlying-delivery settlement are distinct.
Stock settlement historically moved T+3 to T+2 on 2017-09-05 and T+2 to T+1 on
2024-05-28; option premium settlement needs its own applicable schedule.
[APP-C03; APP-C07] Use clearing/banking calendars, not a naive next weekday.
Accrue margin interest, stock borrow, recall/dividend obligations and fees only
from validated dated inputs or an explicitly labeled scenario. Missing borrow
rates are not verified zero. Strict support may prohibit elective borrowed stock
while still representing assignment-generated obligations honestly.

14. P50 MATHEMATICAL DEFINITIONS AND TARGET SEMANTICS

14.1 Exact static portfolio accounting

For signed quantity n_i, multiplier m_i, strike K_i, entry premium e_i:
  C_entry = -sum(n_i*m_i*e_i), unless an exact net entry cash override is supplied.
  V(t,S) = sum(n_i*m_i*v_i(t,S)).
  Pi_gross(t,S) = C_entry + V(t,S).
For an ongoing campaign, replace C_entry by the consistent cumulative gross
cashflow basis and retained exposures; target remains original H0.

Terminal vanilla payoff:
  g_call(S,K)=max(S-K,0); g_put(S,K)=max(K-S,0).
  G(S)=sum(n_i*m_i*g_i(S,K_i)).
  M=C_entry+sup_{S>=0}G(S).
  L=max(0,-C_entry-inf_{S>=0}G(S)).
Compute exact piecewise-linear extrema at S=0, every distinct strike, and tails.
Positive final call-tail slope makes maximum profit infinite; negative makes
loss infinite. Do not replace infinity by a large sampled-grid value. Require
finite M>0 for a NEW half-maximum-profit target. An existing campaign with finite
frozen H0 does not lose that target merely because a replacement position has
unlimited upside; it is evaluated only if its downside, pricing and model domain
are supported. Same-expiry payoff mathematics
cannot certify unsupported mixed-expiry lifecycle portfolios.

14.2 HOLD first-hit event

Let t0 be coherent replay valuation time, T actual economic fixing, t_star the
requested deadline, and A the declared eligible observation times.
  H_close = inf{t>=t0: t in A and Pi_gross(t,X_t)>=H0}.
  p_hold_close(t_star)=Q_path(H_close<=t_star | frozen inputs at t0).
This assumes the current legs are held unchanged. It does not include future
rolls, stop-loss instructions, actual assignments, or risk-desk interventions.
A first touch followed by a loss still counts; endpoint-only probability is not P50.

For a genuinely already achieved eligible theoretical threshold, the hold event
may have probability 1 at t0 under its own definition, but that is not realized
campaign success. A quote/pricer mismatch cannot manufacture such an immediate hit.
Current conditional results do not invent unobserved past touch history.

Historical HOLD deadline validation uses replay t0, never wall-clock launch time.
Require a future eligible supported horizon with t_star<=actual T. If a campaign
hard cutoff extends beyond the current legs' fixing, do not silently clamp the
requested HOLD horizon or reprice expired options; return the supported expiry
reference separately and a reason for the unsupported beyond-expiry request.
Managed values use their explicit finite campaign horizon/extension rules, not
an unlabeled substitution of T_hard for the original requested T0. Show their
comparison horizon next to each benchmark and changed-deadline probability.

14.3 Terminal and endpoint companions

Always maintain distinct fields for:
  hold first-hit by requested deadline;
  closing-only first-hit through the last eligible expiry observation;
  expiry reference including terminal payoff success;
  incremental settlement-only success probability;
  probability Pi(t_star,X_t_star)>=H0 exactly at the deadline;
  terminal POP >=0.01 USD for the whole position;
  optional terminal strictly-positive POP.

At t_star=T, the source's primary reference includes the disclosed settlement
event, with closing-only companion still visible. An endpoint during a closed
session is theoretical and not an executable close. Source first-hit curves,
unconditional hit-interval mass, separate terminal atom, and success-conditioned
mean/median hit time remain part of the supported reporting contract.

14.4 Path model and actual option maturity

Legacy-compatible QQQ HOLD profile:
  dS=(r-q)*S*dt + sigma_path*S*dW.
  S_next=S*exp((r-q-0.5*sigma_path^2)*dt + sigma_path*sqrt(dt)*Z).
  dt=(UTC_epoch_next-UTC_epoch_now)/31536000, ACT/365F.
One underlying path drives all legs; no independent paths by contract.
Diffusion/decay continues through nights/weekends while closing observations are
restricted to eligible times. Every leg is priced with tau=year_fraction(t,T),
never year_fraction(t,t_star). There is no valid generic conversion from expiry
P50 by a DTE fraction, sqrt(time), delta or a single terminal CDF.

Freeze path IV and each leg's compatible IV for a given HOLD query. Today's next
replay snapshot is a new conditional query with new inputs to the SAME pack,
not continuation of an immutable initial-world forecast. Different per-leg IVs
and path IV define a hybrid mark event, not a uniquely calibrated coherent
full-market risk-neutral law. Report Q_path/GBM with frozen-leg-IV repricing;
do not call it empirical real-world probability or verified tastytrade internals. Future premium interest
does not accrue inside the legacy gross HOLD event. Actual account interest and
already-booked carry remain separately accounted; a carry-inclusive forecast
requires a different explicit event/profile.

14.5 Pricing and model-basis checks

QQQ uses an American exercise-aware solver/surrogate under the chosen r/q/IV/expiry.
The offline reference is the SRC-D obstacle-problem finite-difference solver,
with convergence, domain, positivity/generator, intrinsic and boundary checks.
European BSM/Black formulas remain analytic fixtures and conditional adapters,
not a silent replacement for QQQ American prices.

At initialization B0=C_entry+sum(n_i*m_i*model_price_i(t0)). A hypothetical midpoint
entry should reconcile near zero; compare residual with the source's target-based
basis tolerance. An actual bid/ask fill has legitimate spread cost and an existing
position has genuine prior P&L. Separate those from pricer/quote error. Do not
subtract B0 from every future value or refit original cash to erase a loss.
Define the independent quote/model residual as R_model=V_model(t0)-V_mid(t0),
separate from C_entry+V_mid(t0), which contains genuine entry/spread/history P&L.
Reject material impossible marks/static bounds violations; do not clamp a spread
to its width to create a target touch. Save raw/used IV and repricing residuals.

14.6 Managed success is a different event

Managed probability conditions on the exact current campaign and feasible future
actions, execution costs, risk rules, model transitions and finite hard cutoff.
Success requires actually executing a qualifying realization of frozen H0 on
the declared net basis by the allowed deadline, with obligations accounted for.
The policy must make the exit decision; a theoretical touch alone is insufficient.
Compare p0_policy to current comparable policy value, and p0_hold separately.
Changing a deadline/model/objective makes comparison a changed-basis comparison,
not evidence that the original probability was preserved.

Optional extra target percentages accept a scalar/list in PERCENT UNITS: 25 means
25%, converted once to 0.25. Normalize to unique fractions in (0,1], include 50%,
reject bool/nonfinite/invalid input. Campaign auxiliary thresholds use ORIGINAL
M0; they do not use the replacement position's changing maximum profit.
Optional day offsets mean New York calendar days preserving wall time, not trading
days or fixed 24-hour increments; handle DST explicitly, list out-of-horizon
requests without clamping. These additional reports do not reset a campaign H0.

14.7 Greek units and exact-state risk features

Compute model Greeks under the declared frozen-IV pricing profile and authoritative
QQQ spot, keeping raw vendor Greeks separate when their units/basis differ.
Portfolio delta=sum(n_i*m_i*delta_i)+stock_shares, in equivalent QQQ shares.
Portfolio gamma=sum(n_i*m_i*gamma_i), in delta-shares per one-dollar spot move.
Portfolio vega for a one-percentage-point parallel leg-IV shift is
0.01*sum(n_i*m_i*dv_i/dsigma_i), in USD; specify whether a surface-factor vega
uses a different named bump. Theta is USD per stated calendar-day time passage,
with actual remaining expiry/calendar behavior, not an unlabeled vendor unit.
Use exact payoff/tail/cash constraints alongside Greeks. Shape-sampling nodes
and normalization must be fixed in the pack schema, include strike/near-target
sensitivity and stated tail treatment, and never replace exact tail-risk checks.

15. P50 HISTORICAL PARAMETER RESOLUTION AND FAST-QUERY INTEGRATION

15.1 Inputs not guaranteed by the QQQ files

SRC-B/C do not guarantee historical rates, dividend forecasts, contract multipliers/
deliverables, official closes, complete calendars, historical house margin, borrow
rates, or executable depth. Provide a versioned local reference-data/parameter
registry with source, raw units, effective_from/to, known_at, retrieval time and
confidence. Current web/API values must not be used as past-known parameters.
Missing essentials return NEEDS_MODEL_INPUT or a precise error; ordinary chart/
chain inspection remains usable. Explicit dated overrides create SCENARIO_INPUT
results, not a claim of historically observed values.

Historical QQQ rate priority: valid explicit dated override; validated historical
rate observation compatible with the model; otherwise MISSING_RATE. The old live
broker priority is conditional to a future live adapter. American QQQ put-call
prices cannot be substituted into European parity to invent a precise rate.
Normalize validated rates to continuous annual units with tenor/compounding
evidence. Do not infer scale from a decimal-string type alone.

Historical carry: sourced contemporaneously known continuous-yield proxy or a
scoped dated override, under the inherited approximation. Missing dividend data
is not zero. A discrete-dividend production model is a separate explicit model
version with matching path/pricing/event/benchmark changes.

15.2 Historical quote quality profile

Define REPLAY_QQQ_MINUTE_V1 separately from LIVE_RTH/PREVIOUS_SESSION. Use t0=D
from Section 07, raw quote cutoff <D, and actual source precision. Track spot age,
quote age, IV age, quote dispersion and model residual. The source's live 2/10/60
second thresholds remain in opening source block SRC-D; they are not claimed satisfied.

As an explicit inherited starting gate for probability inputs, use SRC-D's prior-
snapshot limits of 300 seconds for option quotes/IV and 60 seconds inter-leg
dispersion, measured at historical t0 using conservative timestamp-interval age
and dispersion bounds. If an event is only known to lie in [M,M+60s), its worst
age at D is D-M; do not add another 60 seconds to that already conservative age.
Require an authoritative current completed QQQ candle;
prior-candle substitution is off by default and, if explicitly enabled, carries
a separate declared maximum age and stale-data status. These are model-input
quality settings, not source-ingestion liquidity filters or measured broker rules.
Validate their suitability on the actual archive before qualifying the profile.

If a timestamp is only a minute label, preserve its uncertainty interval; do not
claim subsecond freshness. Use a conservative age upper bound. A chain can show
staler quotes with labels while strict probability or new fills are unavailable.
No future interpolation, whole-day smoothing or secretly refreshed source time.

15.3 Path IV and frozen leg IV

Resolve path IV from a matching, valid as-of expiration IV if available. Otherwise
use a validated local ATM reference derived from contemporaneous QQQ quotes,
the authoritative spot, correct American pricer, rates/carry and that expiry.
No IV rank, unrelated tenor, historical realized volatility or vendor embedded
underlying substitution. Preserve exact contributing contracts and fit diagnostics.

For each leg, accept vendor IV only if timing/units and quote repricing pass.
Otherwise use a validated as-of IV inversion under the same pricing assumptions.
Intrinsic plateaus/low vega can make IV unidentifiable; return a reason rather than
an arbitrary root. Preserve raw vendor IV and label inferred IV QUOTE_INVERTED.

Online inversion implementation choice: consume prevalidated as-of IV artifacts
or a packed inverse-pricing lookup with bounded deterministic interpolation and
residual/identifiability checks. No hidden American PDE or high-resolution root
search during replay. Exact per-contract IV inversion may occur during explicit
preprocessing/offline oracle work, with inputs restricted to that timestamp and
fingerprinted by raw quote, authoritative spot, r/q and pricer version. This is
pointwise preparation, not fitting a past chain with future rows.

15.4 Fast-query contract

Inputs: immutable coherent snapshot; exact draft/open legs; actual/hypothetical
entry distinction; full campaign ledger; fixed target/horizons; account constraints;
current candidate universe; selected validated pack and rule identities.

Steps: validate -> exact payoff/accounting/risk -> normalize -> domain gate ->
batched hold/value inference -> bounded feasible-action search -> exact proposal
check -> uncertainty gates -> immutable result. Cache only by complete identity.
On new spot/IV/time/legs, pass new inputs through the same pack. Do not use a stale
probability merely because contracts match. A cached pure pricing basis may be
reused at the new coordinates inside its validated support.

Forbidden on this path: Monte Carlo; high-resolution target-region roots; PDE/grid
refinement; global dynamic programming; training; exhaustive market-wide search;
automatic numerical fallback/rebuild. An out-of-domain state returns
OUTSIDE_MODEL_DOMAIN/MODEL_COVERAGE_UNAVAILABLE. It may be logged for a later
explicit rebuild, never silently sent to the expensive legacy engine.

Do not report this part complete until its Section 28 milestones,
corresponding full tests and the above completion gate are evidenced.
